:where(.lgad ol){padding:0;list-style:none}

.lgad-body{padding:22px 26px 20px}
.lgad-q{font-size:20px;line-height:1.3;font-weight:650;letter-spacing:-.01em}

.lgad-step{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;transition:color .25s}
.lgad-step.is-on{color:var(--token-2f94e528-d718-41d5-9b3e-cffb4fc57dff)}
.lgad-step.is-done{color:var(--token-9f277e15-6dea-4896-a90a-946931eaa20d)}
.lgad-box{width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:3px;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1.5px currentColor;position:relative;flex:none;display:inline-block;transition:background-color .2s,box-shadow .2s}
.lgad-step.is-on .lgad-box{box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1.5px var(--token-2f94e528-d718-41d5-9b3e-cffb4fc57dff);background:linear-gradient(var(--token-fe5bff12-dea2-4af8-9b6a-6672bf208b34),var(--token-fe5bff12-dea2-4af8-9b6a-6672bf208b34)) 0 100%/100% 0 no-repeat;animation:lgadFill 1.1s ease-in-out infinite}

.lgad-step.is-done .lgad-box{background:var(--token-2f94e528-d718-41d5-9b3e-cffb4fc57dff);box-shadow:none}
.lgad-step.is-done .lgad-box::after{content:&quot;&quot;;position:absolute;left:5px;top:2.2px;width:3.6px;height:7px;border:solid #fff;border-width:0 1.7px 1.7px 0;transform:rotate(45deg)}
.lgad-a{margin-top:17px;font-size:17px;line-height:1.5;max-width:36em;transition:opacity .45s ease,transform .45s ease}

.lgad-tb th{text-align:left;font-weight:500;color:var(--token-3b95cb06-e051-468c-acd2-a15ec83639a0);padding:6px 0 5px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--token-fec8fc86-3712-4647-8d33-c0bd442a772a)}

.lgad-pop:not(.is-in) .lgad-tb tbody tr{opacity:0;transform:translateX(-4px)}

.lgad-leader.is-in circle{opacity:1}
.lgad[data-layout=&quot;stacked&quot;] .lgad-leader{display:none}
.lgad.is-still *,.lgad.is-still *::after{transition:none!important;animation:none!important}

class="lgdp" style="height:100%;width:100%;--lgdp-ink:var(--token-2f94e528-d718-41d5-9b3e-cffb4fc57dff);--lgdp-paper:rgb(242, 243, 239);--lgdp-marker:var(--token-fe5bff12-dea2-4af8-9b6a-6672bf208b34);--lgdp-accent:var(--token-1b0ab74a-5c30-4b9e-adc6-b03e38e5f059);background:var(--token-2f94e528-d718-41d5-9b3e-cffb4fc57dff)"><style>
.lgdp{position:relative;width:100%;height:100%;overflow:hidden;container-type:inline-size;font-family:&quot;Archivo&quot;,ui-sans-serif,system-ui,-apple-system,&quot;Segoe UI&quot;,Roboto,Arial,sans-serif;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased}
.lgdp-v{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;display:block;cursor:pointer;background:transparent}
.lgdp:fullscreen .lgdp-v{object-fit:contain!important}
.lgdp-ctl{position:absolute;left:16px;right:16px;bottom:20px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;pointer-events:none}
.lgdp-btn{position:relative;pointer-events:auto;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;height:36px;padding:0 14px;border:0;border-radius:6px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.62);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,.2);color:#fff;font:inherit;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;line-height:1;cursor:pointer;transition:background-color .2s,color .2s,box-shadow .2s}
.lgdp-btn::after{content:&quot;&quot;;position:absolute;inset:-4px}
.lgdp-btn:hover{background:var(--lgdp-paper);color:var(--lgdp-ink);box-shadow:none}
.lgdp-btn:focus-visible,.lgdp-play:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--lgdp-marker);outline-offset:2px}
.lgdp-sq{width:36px;padding:0;justify-content:center}
.lgdp-bar{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;height:3px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.16);pointer-events:none}
.lgdp-bar i{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--lgdp-accent);transform-origin:left center;transform:scaleX(0)}
.lgdp-play{position:absolute;left:50%;top:50%;width:88px;height:88px;margin:-44px 0 0 -44px;border:0;border-radius:50%;background:var(--lgdp-marker);color:var(--lgdp-ink);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding-left:6px;cursor:pointer;transition:transform .25s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)}
.lgdp-play:hover{transform:scale(1.06)}
.lgdp-empty{position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:rgba(255,255,255,.6);font-size:14px;text-align:center;padding:24px}
@container (max-width: 520px){
.lgdp-ctl{left:12px;right:12px;bottom:14px}
.lgdp-btn{height:32px;padding:0 11px;font-size:12.5px;gap:6px}
.lgdp-sq{width:32px;padding:0}
